here Form IV selects Jacob as cause-sender rather than Benjamin as autonomous mover","qac_refs":["12:66:3:1","12:66:3:2"],"root":{"arabic":"ر س ل","transliteration":"r-s-l"},"surface":{"arabic":"أُرْسِلَهُۥ","transliteration":"ursilahu"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:4","analytic_gloss_range_en":"with-you accompaniment phrase making the brothers themselves the travel company and risk environment; the root rarity signal is only a tagging artifact","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"QAC assigns a technical root for this common accompaniment word; it should not be treated as a rare lexical root claim","qac_refs":["12:66:4:1","12:66:4:2"],"root":{"arabic":"م ع ع","transliteration":"m-ʿ-ʿ"},"surface":{"arabic":"مَعَكُمْ","transliteration":"maʿakum"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:5","analytic_gloss_range_en":"until-threshold particle that converts refusal into a condition and opens the path from withholding to acceptable pledge delivery","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:5:1"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"حَتَّىٰ","transliteration":"ḥattā"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:6","analytic_gloss_range_en":"Form IV pledge-transfer verb requiring the brothers to deliver a covenant object to Jacob; qiraat variation affects sound and suffix length but not the legal transfer","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"coming, arriving, bringing, giving, and causing something to come; here Form IV selects active transfer of the pledge to Jacob","qac_refs":["12:66:6:1","12:66:6:2","12:66:6:3"],"root":{"arabic":"أ ت ي","transliteration":"ʾ-t-y"},"surface":{"arabic":"تُؤْتُونِ","transliteration":"tuʾtūni"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:7","analytic_gloss_range_en":"an indefinite, accusative binding pledge required as the delivered object, grounded in God and later repeated as the brothers’ possessed pledge","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"firmness, reliability, fastening, binding, and covenant-security; here the root makes the oath a secured, solemn bond rather than loose assurance","qac_refs":["12:66:7:1"],"root":{"arabic":"و ث ق","transliteration":"w-th-q"},"surface":{"arabic":"مَوْثِقًۭا","transliteration":"mawthiqan"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:8","analytic_gloss_range_en":"source or invocation preposition attaching the pledge to divine authority rather than leaving it as a merely human promise","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:8:1"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"مِّنَ","transliteration":"mina"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:9","analytic_gloss_range_en":"genitive divine name anchoring the pledge’s authority, with derivational awe or worship pressure kept subordinate to the proper-name function","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"divinity, worshipped authority, and contested awe/bewilderment derivational pressure; locally the proper name grounds and later oversees the covenant","qac_refs":["12:66:9:1"],"root":{"arabic":"أ ل ه","transliteration":"ʾ-l-h"},"surface":{"arabic":"ٱللَّهِ","transliteration":"llāhi"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:10","analytic_gloss_range_en":"oath-lam introducing and binding the specific sworn content of the pledge to the return verb","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:10:1"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"لَ","transliteration":"la"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:11","analytic_gloss_range_en":"maximally emphatic come-with or bring-back verb requiring the brothers to arrive to Jacob with Benjamin, not merely report about him","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"coming, arriving, bringing, giving, and causing arrival; here Form I with a bi-phrase selects embodied co-arrival with the person returned","qac_refs":["12:66:10:2","12:66:10:3","12:66:10:4"],"root":{"arabic":"أ ت ي","transliteration":"ʾ-t-y"},"surface":{"arabic":"تَأْتُنَّنِى","transliteration":"lataʾtunnanī"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:12","analytic_gloss_range_en":"bi-plus-pronoun completing the come-with-him construction and keeping Benjamin as the tracked object of return","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:11:1","12:66:11:2"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"بِهِۦٓ","transliteration":"bihi"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:13","analytic_gloss_range_en":"exception particle functioning as unless and carving one defined release condition out of the oath","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:12:1"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"إِلَّآ","transliteration":"illā"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:14","analytic_gloss_range_en":"subordinating particle that turns the passive encirclement into the defined event inside the exception","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["12:66:13:1"],"root":{"note":"no root"},"surface":{"arabic":"أَن","transliteration":"an"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"12:66:15","analytic_gloss_range_en":"Form IV passive encirclement or overwhelming enclosure, making total external incapacity the only release condition from the return oath","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"surrounding, encircling, enclosing, encompassing, and by extension total knowledge; HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2dd822adf88506970d3e","text":"Later 12:80 has the eldest brother explicitly recall this covenant when the crisis occurs, confirming that the binding speech remains operative as an internal constraint even when performance fails.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001623/B002","root_001623/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6d2156616bde33115419","text":"1. **Speech fastened into an accountable bond.** `و ث ق:B004` is a firm covenant confirmed by pledge or oath; `و ث ق:B002` is firm fastening and taking the surer course. The father converts a repeated informal guarding claim into a specified return obligation, states one encompassing-force exception, and names God over what they say; 12:67-68 adds precautions while denying those precautions absolute efficacy. The packet supplies promise, exception, and witness; I infer that the covenant fastens agency where control is possible without pretending to bind uncontrollable events. *Mawthiqan min Allāh* reads as bounded accountability.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B002","root_001623/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_986f0d663dd736c43a1d","text":"1. **Trust is converted into a firm, witnessed obligation.** و ث ق B002/B004 (firm fastening; covenant), ء ت ي B002 (bringing), and و ك ل B006 (one who undertakes, suffices, guards) formalize what was previously verbal assurance. I infer that invoking God places the speech under an overseeing guarantor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000372/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a4fdb9469932b83bc003","text":"2. **The exception is total external encirclement.** ح و ط B001/B006 (enclosure; overwhelming encirclement/ruin) defines inability as being surrounded rather than choosing neglect.
